#2836. A Vice City rout: Heat defeat Bucks 137-130 in collab with ‘GTA VI'

A Vice City rout: Heat defeat Bucks 137-130 in collab with 'GTA VI' originally appeared on NBC Sports Boston

A loss on Vice City night never made sense.

The Miami Heat routed the Milwaukee Bucks 137-130 on Wednesday during a one-night-only promotional collaboration with Rockstar's "Grand Theft Auto VI," which launches in the U.S. at midnight Thursday.

Miami rolled out a new court at Kaseya Center with pink, blue and black colors matching its special-edition jersey. The words "Vice City" replaced the usual Heat logo in mid-court, while a "VI" with a palm tree featured in the corner of each paint.

Giannis Antetokounmpo and Bam Adebayo led the way for Miami in a night that meant a lot to both players. Antetokounmpo faced the team that drafted him for the first time, while Adebayo went head-to-head against former teammate Tyler Herro and Co.

Antetokounmpo posted a game-high 27 points on 7 of 11 shooting to go with 13 rebounds, nine assists, one steal and a block.

Adebayo followed suit with 26 points on 7 of 15 shooting to go with four rebounds, two assists and two steals. Adebayo dominated the first half, while Antetokounmpo took over in the second.

Davion Mitchell added 24 points on a hot 6 of 7 clip from long range to help Miami move to 9-6 after a slow start. Milwaukee fell to 1-14 as Kyle Kuzma scored 26 points to lead the team.

Milwaukee closed the gap in the final quarter once Miami pulled most of its starters, but the Heat had been in control since the end of the first.

Beyond the game, fans had access to a limited-edition Vice City merch and apparel collection at team shops throughout the arena. There were also unique cars on display for fans to see and a photo spot with the "VI" logo.

Vice City is the fictional name of the game's city that loosely represents Miami, while Leonida is the fictional Florida-inspired state the city is based in. Other prominent in-game locations are based on the Everglades and the Florida Keys.

"GTA VI" has been expected to be the biggest game of the decade since its initial trailer release in December 2023. The previous installment, based in a fictional Los Angeles and surrounding areas, released 13 years ago.

#2835. 49ers praise ‘off the chain’ Mexican fans for unbelievable support

The 49ers played in the first NFL regular-season game played outside the U.S. in 2005.

San Francisco lost to the Cardinals 31-14 in a historic matchup at Estadio Azteca in Mexico City that season. In 2022, the 49ers returned to the renowned stadium for a rematch with the Cardinals and earned a 38-10 victory.

San Francisco 49ers head coach Kyle Shanahan watches from the sideline in the first half of an NFL football game against the Las Vegas Raiders in Santa Clara, Calif., Sunday, Nov. 8, 2026. AP Photo/Kelley L. Cox

Although it was considered a road game for the 49ers, coach Kyle Shanahan recalled the overwhelming support from Mexican fans.

"It was definitely a home game that week," Shanahan said Wednesday. "I feel like our Mexican fan base is off the chain. They're unbelievable. … The last time we were there, it felt like almost the whole stadium (was filled with 49ers fans)."

San Francisco 49ers running back Christian McCaffrey answers a question after an NFL football game against the Dallas Cowboys in Arlington, Texas, Nov. 15, 2026. AP Photo/Julio Cortez

Running back Christian McCaffrey echoed a similar sentiment to Shanahan's, sharing the team is excited to return to Estadio Azteca — since renamed Estadio Banorte — for the highly touted NFC showdown against Minnesota.

"It was one of the coolest experiences playing there," McCaffrey said of the 2022 game in Mexico City.

"That many people being that loud for that long was something that was really special. You never know what to expect going into an international game and unfamiliar territory, but they made it so fun for us. The Mexican fans are unbelievable. So we can't wait to get down there."

San Francisco 49ers fans cheer before an NFL football game against the Dallas Cowboys in Arlington, Texas, Sunday, Nov. 15, 2026. AP Photo/Jeffrey McWhorter

This time, the 49ers are the home team on paper, too. Sportsbooks have had San Francisco favored by three points against the Vikings.

Kickoff from Mexico City is set for 5:20 p.m. PT Sunday, Nov. 22, on NBC and Peacock.

#2834. Darius Acuff Jr.’s historic feat wasted in Kings’ 13th loss of the season

Darius Acuff Jr.'s historic feat wasted in Kings' 13th loss of the season appeared first on ClutchPoints. Add ClutchPoints as a Preferred Source by clicking here.

The Sacramento Kings may be a walking losing streak. But that is not due to any fault from rookie guard Darius Acuff Jr. Acuff has brought it for the Kings on every single night, and he had himself another brilliant outing despite their 128-107 loss to the Phoenix Suns on Wednesday.

Acuff has hit the ground running to begin his NBA career. He was the Kings' best player on the night yet again, finishing with 36 points on 11-of-22 shooting from the field to go along with four rebounds and six assists. He even added two blocks for good measure.

In fact, according to StatMuse, Acuff became just the third rookie since 2000 to record at least 36 points, six assists, and two blocks — joining Kevin Durant and Cooper Flagg in that exclusive club.

Watch sports LIVE with fuboTV (free trial)

It's important to note that both Durant and Flagg won the Rookie of the Year award, and with the eye-popping stats Acuff puts up on a regular basis, it would be surprising if he doesn't bag home the award by the end of the season.

With this latest outing, Acuff is now averaging 26.2 points, 3.6 rebounds, and 7.0 assists per contest on the season — numbers that only true superstars in the NBA can put up.

Now, Acuff is not on that level quite yet. He's benefitted immensely from playing for a Kings team that is going nowhere fast and can afford to have him commandeer the offense despite his inexperience.

Moreover, the Kings are all-in on his development, and him learning to be this incredible scoring and playmaking threat at such a young age bodes well for his superstar trajectory moving forward.

Of course, the Kings are a terrible team all around, having won just one of their first 14 games this season. Be that as it may, they at least have a ray of hope to cling on to considering how well Acuff has been playing to start his NBA career.
